     7	import { canonical, recompute, sealPayload, sha256, verifySeal, type Payload } from '../../packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts';
     8	
     9	const saved = (): Payload => JSON.parse(readFileSync(EVIDENCE, 'utf8'));
    10	const node = (args: string[], env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {}) => execFileSync(process.execPath, ['--no-warnings', 'tools/opponents/assay.ts', ...args], { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, VITEST: '', ...env }, maxBuffer: 1 << 28 });
    11	
    12	describe('opponents evidence', () => {
    13	  it('evidence seal and manifest verify', () => {
    14	    expect(checkSaved()).toEqual([]);
    15	    const p = saved();
    16	    expect(verifySeal(p)).toBe(true);
    17	    for (const s of ['CPU only', 'no GPU evidence', 'not the M3 environment']) expect(p.statements.some((x) => x.includes(s))).toBe(true);
    18	    expect(JSON.stringify(p)).not.toMatch(/generatedAt|platform|darwin|linux/);
    19	    expect(['assay-sensitive', 'label-or-position-biased', 'insensitive', 'extinction-limited', 'control-invalid']).toContain(p.verdict);
    20	  });
    21	
    22	  it('evidence records seeds, panel hex, distances, calibration constants, excluded counts, and the naive comparison', () => {
    23	    const p = saved();
    24	    expect(p.config.seeds).toHaveLength(10);
    25	    expect(Object.keys(p.panel.hex)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['G', 'F1', 'F2', 'F3', 'W', "G'"]));
    26	    expect(p.panel.hex["G'"]).toBe(p.panel.hex.G);
    27	    expect(Object.keys(p.panel.distances)).toHaveLength(10);
    28	    expect(p.constants.floorDiv).toBe(16);
    29	    const cal = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION, 'utf8'));
    30	    expect(cal.effectTarget).toBe(p.config.effectTarget);
    31	    expect(cal.selected.tile).toBe(p.config.arena.tile);
    32	    expect(cal.attempts.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(36);
    33	    for (const s of Object.values(p.summary.balanced)) { expect(s[3]).toHaveProperty('excluded'); expect(s[3]).toHaveProperty('naiveMean'); }
    34	    expect(p.summary.twin!.naiveDecisiveRate).not.toBeUndefined();
    35	    expect(p.fixtures.rpsSynthetic.synthetic).toBe(true);
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  it('verdict in evidence equals the verdict recomputed from the stored gates and raw runs', () => {
    39	    const p = saved();
    40	    expect(recompute(p)).toEqual([]);
    41	    expect(p.gates.verdict).toBe(p.verdict);
    42	  });
    43	
    44	  it('a tampered payload, manifest or source is rejected', () => {
    45	    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'opp-'));
    46	    const ev = join(dir, 'evidence.json'), mf = join(dir, 'manifest.json');
    47	    const p = saved();
    48	    p.runs[0].cp[3][0] += 1;
    49	    writeFileSync(ev, JSON.stringify(p, null, 1) + '\n');
    50	    copyFileSync(MANIFEST, mf);
    51	    expect(checkSaved(ev, mf).some((x) => x.includes('integritySha256'))).toBe(true);
    52	    // a manifest hash edited
    53	    const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(MANIFEST, 'utf8'));
    54	    m.payloadFileSha256 = '0'.repeat(64);
    55	    writeFileSync(mf, JSON.stringify(m));
    56	    expect(checkSaved(EVIDENCE, mf).some((x) => x.includes('payloadFileSha256'))).toBe(true);
    57	    // a source tree with one changed file, and an upstream input (the generalist genome source)
    58	    const root = join(dir, 'root') + '/';
    59	    for (const f of sourceFiles()) { mkdirSync(dirname(root + f), { recursive: true }); copyFileSync(REPO + f, root + f); }
    60	    expect(checkSaved(EVIDENCE, MANIFEST, root)).toEqual([]);
    61	    expect(sourceFiles()).toContain('packages/schema/src/genome.ts');
    62	    writeFileSync(root + 'packages/schema/src/genome.ts', readFileSync(REPO + 'packages/schema/src/genome.ts', 'utf8') + '\n// edit\n');
    63	    expect(checkSaved(EVIDENCE, MANIFEST, root).some((x) => x.includes('genome.ts changed since'))).toBe(true);
    64	    copyFileSync(REPO + 'packages/schema/src/genome.ts', root + 'packages/schema/src/genome.ts');
    65	    writeFileSync(root + 'packages/metrics/src/opponents/extra.ts', 'export const x = 1;\n');
    66	    expect(checkSaved(EVIDENCE, MANIFEST, root).some((x) => x.includes('extra.ts is not in the manifest'))).toBe(true);
    67	  });
    68	
    69	  it('a correctly resealed but internally inconsistent payload is rejected', () => {
    70	    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'opp-'));
    71	    const ev = join(dir, 'evidence.json'), mf = join(dir, 'manifest.json');
    72	    const p = saved();
    73	    // edit a summary number and the verdict, recompute the seal, and rewrite the manifest hashes to match
    74	    p.summary.matrix.cycles = [['G', 'F1', 'F2']];
    75	    p.verdict = p.verdict === 'assay-sensitive' ? 'insensitive' : 'assay-sensitive';
    76	    const resealed = sealPayload(p);
    77	    const text = JSON.stringify(resealed, null, 1) + '\n';
    78	    writeFileSync(ev, text);
    79	    const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(MANIFEST, 'utf8'));
    80	    m.payloadFileSha256 = sha256(text);
    81	    m.integritySha256 = resealed.integritySha256;
    82	    m.verdict = resealed.verdict;
    83	    writeFileSync(mf, JSON.stringify(m));
    84	    const problems = checkSaved(ev, mf);
    85	    expect(problems.some((x) => x.includes('stored summary differs') || x.includes('stored verdict'))).toBe(true);
    86	    expect(canonical(p)).not.toBe(canonical(saved()));
    87	  });
    88	
    89	  it('frozen config file matches the saved payload and the calibration file exists', () => {
    90	    expect(canonical(saved().config)).toBe(canonical(CONFIG));
    91	    expect(existsSync(CALIBRATION)).toBe(true);
    92	  });
    93	
    94	  it('the CLI check runs as a subprocess', () => {
    95	    expect(node(['--check'])).toContain('--check ok');
    96	  }, 120_000);
    97	
    98	  it('workers resolve @bl/* without vitest aliases', () => {
    99	    const out = node(['--mini', '--print', '--jobs', '2']);
   100	    const p = JSON.parse(out) as Payload;
   101	    expect(p.runs.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
   102	    expect(verifySeal(p)).toBe(true);
   103	  }, 240_000);
   104	
   105	  it('worker count does not change output; mini regeneration equals itself', () => {
   106	    const a = node(['--mini', '--print', '--jobs', '1']);
   107	    const b = node(['--mini', '--print', '--jobs', '3']);
   108	    expect(a).toBe(b);
   109	    expect(node(['--mini', '--print', '--jobs', '2'])).toBe(a);
   110	  }, 600_000);
   111	});
     1	import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
     2	import { CH, G, genomeDistance, genomeFromHex, genomeHex, lowbias32, stateHash, w2, OUT } from '@bl/schema';
     3	import { RefSim } from '@bl/sim-ref';
     4	import {
     5	  ARMS, MASKS, evaluateGates, panelPairs, runFixtures, schedule, summarize, twinOf, type AssayConfig, type RunRec,
     6	} from '../src/opponents/evidence.ts';
     7	import {
     8	  assertNonOverlap, buildContestWorld, checkpointSteps, readLabels, runJob, slotCentres, PLANT, type ArenaParams, type Job,
     9	} from '../src/opponents/arena.ts';
    10	import { buildPanel, farthestPoints, generalist, weaken } from '../src/opponents/panel.ts';
    11	import {
    12	  EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS, atFloor, blockPayoff, breadth, classify, decideVerdict, edgesFromPayoffs, findCycles, shareA, sharePpm, signFlipP, summarizeInvasion,
    13	  summarizePair, tInterval, vigourExpected, vigourResidual, minP, type Gates, type BlockPayoff, type Verdict,
    14	} from '../src/opponents/payoff.ts';
    15	
    16	const P: ArenaParams = { tile: 32, radius: 3, steps: 40 };
    17	const panel = buildPanel(50);
    18	const job = (seed: number, a: string, b: string | null, sa: number[], sb: number[]): Job => ({ key: 'k', seed, hexA: panel.hex[a as 'G'], hexB: b ? panel.hex[b as 'G'] : null, slotsA: sa, slotsB: sb });
    19	const blocks = (vals: (number | null)[]): BlockPayoff[] => vals.map((v, i) => ({ seed: i, payoff: v, naive: v ?? 0.5, shares: [v] }));
    20	
    21	describe('arena and inoculum', () => {
    22	  it('inoculum is identical across contests: same slot, same step-zero inventory', () => {
    23	    const inv = (j: Job) => { const s = buildContestWorld(j, P); const n = 32 * 32; let b = 0, e = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { b += s.cells[CH.B * n + i]; e += s.cells[CH.E * n + i]; } return [b, e]; };
    24	    const slot0 = (j: Job) => { const s = buildContestWorld(j, P); const n = 1024; const c = slotCentres(32, 7)[0]; return s.cells[CH.B * n + c.y * 32 + c.x]; };
    25	    const a = job(7, 'G', 'F1', [0, 1], [2, 3]);
    26	    const b = job(7, 'G', 'W', [0, 1], [2, 3]);
    27	    const solo = job(7, 'F2', null, [0, 1], []);
    28	    expect(inv(a)).toEqual(inv(b));
    29	    expect(slot0(a)).toBe(slot0(b));
    30	    expect(slot0(a)).toBe(slot0(solo));
    31	    // swapped ratio 1:3 conserves the totals of the 3-slot union
    32	    const r1 = inv(job(7, 'G', 'F1', [0], [1, 2, 3])), r2 = inv(job(7, 'G', 'F1', [1, 2, 3], [0]));
    33	    expect(r1).toEqual(r2);
    34	  });
    35	
    36	  it('step-zero inventory of a slot is equal across contests and solo arenas; reciprocal arenas have equal resource fields', () => {
    37	    const n = 1024;
    38	    const ca = slotCentres(32, 9);
    39	    const sA = buildContestWorld(job(9, 'G', 'F1', [0, 1], [2, 3]), P);
    40	    const sB = buildContestWorld(job(9, 'F1', 'G', [0, 1], [2, 3]), P);
    41	    const sSolo = buildContestWorld(job(9, 'G', null, [2], []), P);
    42	    const at = (s: typeof sA, slot: number) => s.cells[CH.B * n + ca[slot].y * 32 + ca[slot].x];
    43	    expect(at(sA, 2)).toBe(at(sSolo, 2));
    44	    expect(at(sA, 0)).toBe(at(sB, 0));
    45	    const field = (s: typeof sA, ch: number) => Array.from(s.cells.subarray(ch * n, (ch + 1) * n)).join(',');
    46	    expect(field(sA, CH.A)).toBe(field(sB, CH.A));
    47	    expect(field(sA, CH.A)).toBe(field(sSolo, CH.A));
    48	    // emptied slots hold no biomass, energy or lineage
    49	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    50	      const lin = sSolo.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
    51	      if (sSolo.cells[CH.B * n + i] === 0) expect(sSolo.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | lin).toBe(0);
    52	    }
    53	  });
    54	
    55	  it('jitter is reproducible and differs per slot and axis (one-argument lowbias32)', () => {
    56	    expect(slotCentres(32, 5)).toEqual(slotCentres(32, 5));
    57	    const seen = new Set<string>();
    58	    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 30; seed++) for (const c of slotCentres(32, seed)) seen.add(`${c.x},${c.y}`);
    59	    expect(seen.size).toBeGreaterThan(25);
    60	    expect(slotCentres(32, 1)).not.toEqual(slotCentres(32, 2));
    61	    expect(lowbias32.length).toBe(1);
    62	    const c = slotCentres(32, 3);
    63	    for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) expect(Math.abs(c[s].x - [8, 24, 24, 8][s])).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
    64	    assertNonOverlap(32, 3);
    65	    expect(() => assertNonOverlap(32, 7)).toThrow();
    66	  });
    67	
    68	  it('reciprocal positions swap slot sets: relabelling reproduces the same world', () => {
    69	    const g = job(4, 'G', 'F1', ARMS.bal.P1.A.slice(), ARMS.bal.P1.B.slice());
    70	    const h = job(4, 'F1', 'G', ARMS.bal.P2.A.slice(), ARMS.bal.P2.B.slice());
    71	    expect(stateHash(buildContestWorld(g, P))).toBe(stateHash(buildContestWorld(h, P)));
    72	  });
    73	
    74	  it('mutation off: no new lineage ids; labels sum to total biomass', () => {
    75	    const j = job(3, 'G', 'F1', [0, 1], [2, 3]);
    76	    const sim = new RefSim(buildContestWorld(j, P));
    77	    for (const step of checkpointSteps(80)) {
    78	      sim.run(step - sim.state.step);
    79	      const r = readLabels(sim.state, j); // throws on a foreign lineage or an unlabelled biomass unit
    80	      let total = 0; const n = 1024;
    81	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) total += sim.state.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    82	      expect(r.bA + r.bB).toBe(total);
    83	    }
    84	    expect(sim.state.cfg.mutRate).toBe(0);
    85	  });
    86	
    87	  it('readouts do not change physics', () => {
    88	    const j = job(2, 'G', 'W', [0, 1], [2, 3]);
    89	    const hashes: string[] = [];
    90	    runJob(j, { ...P, steps: 40 }, (sim) => { hashes.push(stateHash(sim.state)); });
    91	    const twin = new RefSim(buildContestWorld(j, { ...P, steps: 40 }));
    92	    const plain: string[] = [];
    93	    for (const t of checkpointSteps(40)) { twin.run(t - twin.state.step); plain.push(stateHash(twin.state)); }
    94	    expect(hashes).toEqual(plain);
    95	  });
    96	
    97	  it('twin genomes under two labels are distinguishable by lineage only', () => {
    98	    const j = job(2, 'G', 'G', [0, 1], [2, 3]);
    99	    const s = buildContestWorld(j, P);
   100	    const n = 1024;
   101	    const words = new Map<number, string>();
   102	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   103	      const lo = s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
   104	      if (!lo) continue;
   105	      const w = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, k) => s.genome[(G.W0 + k) * n + i]).join(',') + `|${s.genome[G.PARAM0 * n + i]}`;
   106	      words.set(lo, w);
   107	    }
   108	    expect(words.size).toBe(4);
   109	    expect(new Set(words.values()).size).toBe(1);
   110	    expect(readLabels(s, j).bA).toBeGreaterThan(0);
   111	    expect(twinOf('G')).toBe("G'");
   112	  });
   113	
   114	  it('twin: label swap gives exact count identity', () => {
   115	    const a = runJob({ ...job(5, 'G', 'G', [0, 1], [2, 3]), key: 'a' }, P);
   116	    const b = runJob({ ...job(5, 'G', 'G', [2, 3], [0, 1]), key: 'b' }, P);
   117	    expect([a.b0A, a.b0B]).toEqual([b.b0B, b.b0A]);
   118	    a.cp.forEach((c, k) => { expect([c.bA, c.bB, c.cellsA, c.cellsB]).toEqual([b.cp[k].bB, b.cp[k].bA, b.cp[k].cellsB, b.cp[k].cellsA]); });
   119	  });
   120	});
   121	
   122	describe('panel', () => {
   123	  it('weakened variant differs from parent only in the stated weight', () => {
   124	    const g = generalist(), w = weaken(g, 50);
   125	    const diff: number[] = [];
   126	    g.weights.forEach((v, i) => { if (v !== w.weights[i]) diff.push(i); });
   127	    expect(diff).toEqual([w2(1, OUT.PHOTO)]);
   128	    expect(w.weights[diff[0]]).toBe(55);
   129	    expect(w.mu).toBe(g.mu); expect(w.sigma).toBe(g.sigma);
   130	    expect(genomeDistance(g, w)).toBe(1);
   131	    expect(genomeFromHex(genomeHex(w)).weights).toEqual(w.weights);
   132	  });
   133	  it('farthest-point pick is deterministic and distinct', () => {
   134	    const a = buildPanel(50), b = buildPanel(50);
   135	    expect(a.founderIndices).toEqual(b.founderIndices);
   136	    expect(new Set(a.founderIndices).size).toBe(3);
   137	    for (const [k, d] of Object.entries(a.distances)) if (!k.includes('W') || !k.includes('G')) expect(d, k).toBeGreaterThan(10);
   138	    expect(farthestPoints(generalist(), [generalist(), weaken(generalist(), 90)], 2)).toHaveLength(2);
   139	  });
   140	});
   141	
   142	describe('share arithmetic and classes', () => {
   143	  it('share arithmetic is integer and exact; 0/0 is both-floor, never 0.5', () => {
   144	    expect(shareA(1000, 1000, 0, 0)).toBeNull();
   145	    expect(classify(1000, 1000, 0, 0)).toBe('both-floor');
   146	    expect(shareA(1000, 1000, 600, 200)).toBe(0.75);
   147	    expect(sharePpm(1, 2)).toBe(333333);
   148	    expect(sharePpm(0, 0)).toBeNull();
   149	  });
   150	  it('flooring never creates 0.4999995: gates use counts, ppm is display only', () => {
   151	    const s1 = shareA(1, 1, 3, 3)!, s2 = shareA(1, 1, 3, 3)!;
   152	    expect((s1 + s2) / 2).toBe(0.5);
   153	    expect(sharePpm(1000001, 1000000 + 1000002 - 1000001 + 1)).toBeLessThan(500000 + 1);
   154	  });
   155	  it('run classes at the floor: contested / decisive / both-floor at the threshold edges', () => {
   156	    expect(atFloor(62, 1000)).toBe(true);
   157	    expect(atFloor(63, 1000)).toBe(false);
   158	    expect(classify(1000, 1000, 62, 62)).toBe('both-floor');
   159	    expect(classify(1000, 1000, 62, 63)).toBe('decisive');
   160	    expect(classify(1000, 1000, 63, 63)).toBe('contested');
   161	    expect(shareA(1000, 1000, 62, 63)).toBeCloseTo(62 / 125);
   162	  });
   163	  it('complete-block eligibility drops the whole block when one position is both-floor', () => {
   164	    const live = { b0A: 1000, b0B: 1000, bA: 500, bB: 500 }, dead = { b0A: 1000, b0B: 1000, bA: 1, bB: 1 };
   165	    const b = blockPayoff(1, [live, dead]);
   166	    expect(b.payoff).toBeNull();
   167	    expect(b.naive).toBe(0.5 * (0.5 + 0.5));
   168	    expect(blockPayoff(2, [live, live]).payoff).toBe(0.5);
   169	  });
   170	});
   171	
   172	describe('inference and aggregation', () => {
   173	  it('sign-flip p values: n=6 min 0.031, n=10 min 0.002; edges refused when n < 9', () => {
   174	    expect(signFlipP([1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1])).toBeCloseTo(2 / 64);
   175	    expect(minP(6)).toBeCloseTo(0.03125);
   176	    expect(signFlipP(Array(10).fill(0.3))).toBeCloseTo(2 / 1024);
   177	    expect(signFlipP([])).toBeNull();
   178	    const eight = summarizePair(blocks(Array(8).fill(0.9)), 0.8);
   179	    expect(eight.allSameSide).toBe(true);
   180	    expect(eight.edge).toBeNull();
   181	    expect(summarizePair(blocks(Array(9).fill(0.9)), 0.8).edge).toBe('A>B');
   182	    expect(summarizePair(blocks(Array(10).fill(0.1))).edge).toBe('B>A');
   183	    expect(EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS).toBe(9);
   184	  });
   185	  it('payoff interval from known blocks', () => {
   186	    const t = tInterval([0.4, 0.5, 0.6])!;
   187	    expect(t.mean).toBeCloseTo(0.5);
   188	    expect(t.df).toBe(2);
   189	    expect(t.hi - t.mean).toBeCloseTo(4.303 * 0.1 / Math.sqrt(3), 6);
   190	    expect(tInterval([0.5])).toBeNull();
   191	  });
   192	  it('vigour-only fixture has zero residual', () => {
   193	    const e = vigourExpected(300, 100);
   194	    expect(e).toBe(0.75);
   195	    expect(vigourResidual(0.75, e)).toBe(0);
   196	    expect(vigourExpected(0, 0)).toBeNull();
   197	    expect(runFixtures().vigourOnly.pass).toBe(true);
   198	  });
   199	  it('cycle detector finds the constructed cycle only', () => {
   200	    const f = runFixtures();
   201	    expect(f.rpsSynthetic.cycles).toEqual([['A', 'B', 'C']]);
   202	    expect(f.transitive.cycles).toEqual([]);
   203	    expect(f.straddle.edge).toBeNull();
   204	    expect(f.pass).toBe(true);
   205	    const e = edgesFromPayoffs([[null, 0.8, 0.2], [0.2, null, 0.8], [0.8, 0.2, null]]);
   206	    expect(findCycles(e)).toEqual([[0, 1, 2]]);
   207	    expect(findCycles(edgesFromPayoffs([[null, 0.52, 0.5], [0.48, null, 0.5], [0.5, 0.5, null]]))).toEqual([]);
   208	  });
   209	  it('asymmetric exclusion fixtures behave as stated', () => {
   210	    expect(runFixtures().asymmetricExclusion).toMatchObject({ complete: 7, excluded: 3, status: 'uninformative', pass: true });
   211	  });
   212	  it('breadth is separate from mean payoff', () => {
   213	    // X beats two weak opponents narrowly, Y beats one strong opponent hard; equal mean payoff, different breadth
   214	    const e = [[false, true, true, false], [false, false, false, false], [false, false, false, false], [false, false, false, false]];
   215	    expect(breadth(e)).toEqual([2, 0, 0, 0]);
   216	    const mean = (xs: number[]) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length;
   217	    expect(mean([0.6, 0.6, 0.5])).toBeCloseTo(mean([0.9, 0.5, 0.3]));
   218	  });
   219	  it('invasion from rare labels: invades, fails, unclear, uninformative', () => {
   220	    expect(summarizeInvasion([0.1, 0.2, 0.05, 0.1, 0.3, 0.1]).class).toBe('invades');
   221	    expect(summarizeInvasion([-0.1, -0.2, -0.05, -0.1, -0.3, -0.1]).class).toBe('fails');
   222	    expect(summarizeInvasion([0.1, -0.2, 0.05, 0.1, 0.3, 0.1]).class).toBe('unclear');
   223	    expect(summarizeInvasion([0.1, null, null, 0.1, 0.3, 0.1]).class).toBe('uninformative');
   224	  });
   225	  it('rare arms: neutral expectation is the measured initial share', () => {
   226	    // a rare label that holds exactly its initial share has excess 0 even though that share is far from 0.5
   227	    const cfg: AssayConfig = { arena: P, depletion: null, weakenPercent: 50, ids: ['G', 'W'], seeds: [1, 2], rareSeeds: [1, 2], rarePositions: 1, twinG: true, effectTarget: 1 };
   228	    const runs: RunRec[] = schedule(cfg).map((j) => {
   229	      const solo = j.key[0] === 's';
   230	      const b0: [number, number] = solo ? [1000, 0] : [Math.round(1000 * j.slotsA.length), Math.round(1000 * j.slotsB.length)];
   231	      return { key: j.key, b0, e0: [0, 0], cp: [0, 1, 2, 3].map(() => [b0[0], b0[1], 1, 1] as [number, number, number, number]) };
   232	    });
   233	    const s = summarize(cfg, runs, [10, 20, 30, 40]);
   234	    expect(s.invasion['G|W']!.rareA.excess.every((v) => v === 0)).toBe(true);
   235	    expect(s.invasion['G|W']!.rareA.class).not.toBe('invades');
   236	  });
   237	});
   238	
   239	describe('schedule, verdict', () => {
   240	  it('schedule counts match the plan', () => {
   241	    const cfg: AssayConfig = JSON.parse(require('node:fs').readFileSync('tools/opponents/config.json', 'utf8'));
   242	    const jobs = schedule(cfg);
   243	    expect(panelPairs(cfg.ids)).toHaveLength(10);
   244	    expect(jobs.filter((j) => j.key.startsWith('c|') && j.key.includes('|bal|'))).toHaveLength(11 * 10 * 2);
   245	    expect(jobs.filter((j) => j.key.startsWith('s|'))).toHaveLength(5 * (2 * 10 + 4 * 6));
   246	    expect(jobs.filter((j) => j.depletion)).toHaveLength(20);
   247	    expect(new Set(jobs.map((j) => j.key)).size).toBe(jobs.length);
   248	    expect(Object.keys(MASKS)).toHaveLength(6);
   249	  });
   250	  it('verdict decision table is exhaustive and ordered', () => {
   251	    const bools = [false, true];
   252	    let n = 0;
   253	    for (const G1a of bools) for (const G1b of bools) for (const G2 of bools) for (const G3 of bools) for (const G4 of bools) for (const G5 of bools) for (const invalid of bools) {
   254	      const g: Gates = { G1a, G1b, G2, G3, G4, G5, invalid };
   255	      const want: Verdict = invalid || !G4 || !G5 ? 'control-invalid' : !G1a || !G1b ? 'label-or-position-biased' : !G2 ? 'insensitive' : !G3 ? 'extinction-limited' : 'assay-sensitive';
   256	      expect(decideVerdict(g)).toBe(want);
   257	      n++;
   258	    }
   259	    expect(n).toBe(128);
   260	  });
   261	  it('verdict follows gates', () => {
   262	    const ok: Gates = { G1a: true, G1b: true, G2: true, G3: true, G4: true, G5: true, invalid: false };
   263	    expect(decideVerdict(ok)).toBe('assay-sensitive');
   264	    expect(decideVerdict({ ...ok, G1b: false, G2: false })).toBe('label-or-position-biased');
   265	    expect(decideVerdict({ ...ok, G2: false, G3: false })).toBe('insensitive');
   266	    expect(decideVerdict({ ...ok, G3: false })).toBe('extinction-limited');
   267	    expect(decideVerdict({ ...ok, G4: false, G1a: false })).toBe('control-invalid');
   268	  });
   269	  it('depletion arena is both-floor and the naive reading says tie (classifier path)', () => {
   270	    const f = runFixtures().bothFloorClassifier;
   271	    expect(f.cls).toBe('both-floor');
   272	    expect(f.share).toBeNull();
   273	    expect(f.naiveShare).toBe(0.5);  });
   274	});

   105	## 5. Tests (vitest, named cases)
   106	
   107	`packages/metrics/test/opponents.test.ts`
   108	- `inoculum is identical across contests`: every contest and solo run at fixed seed has the same disc count, biomass and energy totals; ratio 1:3 swaps conserve totals.
   109	- `reciprocal positions swap slot sets`: swapping labels A/B reproduces the same world with labels exchanged (state hash equal after relabelling).
   110	- `mutation off: no new lineage ids`: 400 steps, every living cell's lineage in the founder set at each checkpoint.
   111	- `labels sum to total biomass`: labelled biomass equals total CH.B.
   112	- `readouts do not change physics`: instrumented vs plain twin `stateHash` equal.
   113	- `twin genomes under two labels are distinguishable by lineage only`: identical genome words, different lineage ids, map resolves.
   114	- `share arithmetic is integer and exact`: ppm values for hand cases including 0/0 -> class `both-floor` (never 0.5).
   115	- `run classes at the floor`: contested / decisive / both-floor from hand-built biomass triples at the threshold edges.
   116	- `depletion arena is both-floor and the naive reading says tie`: the calibrated depletion arena (or, on fallback, a hand-built both-floor state); sealed class `both-floor`; naive share 0.5 asserted in the same test; raw inventories of both labels asserted below the floor.
   117	- `payoff interval from known blocks`: hand-computed mean and t interval for fixed per-block values.
   118	- `vigour-only fixture has zero residual`: 3:1 vigour, payoff = 0.75, residual 0.
   119	- `cycle detector finds the constructed cycle only`: `rps-synthetic` yields exactly one cycle (as a set of three), the transitive matrix yields none, a matrix with an interval straddling 0.5 yields no edge.
   120	- `invasion from rare labels`: hand blocks give `invades`, `fails`, `unclear`.
   121	- `breadth is separate from mean payoff`: two genotypes with equal mean payoff and different breadth.
   122	- `jitter is reproducible and differs per slot and axis` (one-argument `lowbias32`).
   123	- `step-zero inventory of a slot is equal across contests and solo arenas`; `reciprocal arenas have equal resource fields`.
   124	- `twin: label swap gives exact count identity; flooring never creates 0.4999995` (ppm display only).
   125	- `complete-block eligibility drops the whole block when one position is both-floor`.
   126	- `rare arms: neutral expectation is the measured initial share`.
   127	- `sign-flip p values: n=6 min 0.031, n=10 min 0.002; edges refused when n < 9`.
   128	- `verdict decision table is exhaustive` (every gate combination incl. G4/G5 failed -> control-invalid).
   129	- `farthest-point pick is deterministic and distinct`: picks stable, pairwise `genomeDistance` above the clustering distance.
   130	- `weakened variant differs from parent only in the stated weight`.
   131	- `verdict follows gates`: synthetic gate sets give each verdict (`assay-sensitive`, `label-or-position-biased`, `insensitive`, `extinction-limited`) and the precedence order is fixed.
   132	
   133	`tools/test/opponents-evidence.test.ts`
   134	- `evidence seal and manifest verify` (`checkSaved()` returns no problems; no `generatedAt`/platform strings; statements include "CPU only", "no GPU evidence", "not the M3 environment").
   135	- `a tampered payload, manifest or source is rejected` (edit a payoff, a manifest hash, one source file, an upstream input such as `generalistGenome` source).
   136	- `workers resolve @bl/* without vitest aliases` (Node 24 subprocess, VITEST unset).
   137	- `a correctly resealed but internally inconsistent payload is rejected` (summary or gate edited, seal and manifest recomputed).
   138	- `worker count does not change output`: a mini config (2 pairs, 2 seeds, T=60) at `--jobs 1` and `--jobs 3` is byte equal.
   139	- `mini regeneration equals itself` (determinism).
   140	- `evidence records seeds, panel hex, distances, calibration constants, excluded counts, and the naive comparison`.
   141	- `verdict in evidence equals the verdict recomputed from the stored gates`.
   142	
   143	`apps/lab/test/opponents-packaging.test.ts` (same shape as the predictability one): Vite input present, Dockerfile removal list and Caddy private route (`/opponents`, `/OPPONENTS/index.html` match; `/opponents-other` does not), page text carries "CPU only", "no GPU evidence", "noindex", a captioned `<table>`, and the site build keeps a self-contained asset graph after the Dockerfile deletions.
   144	
   145	`apps/lab/test/opponents-render.test.ts`: matrix renders the same numbers as the table; `uninformative` cells are labelled, not coloured as ties; reduced-motion; checkpoint/ratio/position toggles change the displayed numbers; text says synthetic for the cycle fixture.

   161	
   162	## 7. Page content
   163	
   164	Header "Local experiment / historical opponents, first rung". Plain-language option text for ratio ("1:1 even start; 1:3 one side starts rare, which tests invasion"), position ("both positions averaged; the difference is shown as position effect") and contest time. Matrix cells show payoff, interval and excluded count; `uninformative` is its own state. Caption "What this does not show": a sensitivity check on five genetically separated founders in small CPU arenas, not the M3 environment; a payoff is share of biomass at a step, not reproduction or fitness; wins can be vigour, see the vigour toggle; the cycle fixture is synthetic; "none detected" is not "none exist"; no archive tournament has been run; CPU only, no GPU evidence, not on the public site. A real `<table>` carries every number the canvas shows.
   165	
   166	## 8. Non-goals and limits
   167	
   168	- No historical-archive tournament, no evolving against opponents, no generality-on-withheld-opponents (needs training history), no synthesis-versus-lottery attribution (reported as unresolved, no predation claim), no whole-patch transfer (that moves stored resources and developmental state; different experiment).
   169	- No physics, schema, preset or `RULE_VERSION` change; no GPU or backend qualification; hosted/physical-GPU evidence: none.
   170	- Small `sim-ref` worlds, five genotypes, S=10 seed blocks (6 for rare arms): wide intervals are expected and stated; an interval containing 0.5 is not evidence of equality.
   171	- Weakened variant is a single hand-built manipulation, a sensitivity floor, not a spectrum of effect sizes.
   172	- Fitness here is relative biomass share at a fixed step; it does not establish organism reproduction, open-endedness or ecological context effects.
   173	- No paid calls, AWS, deploy, push or PR at this stage; the plan stage commits nothing.
   174	
   175	## 9. Size and order of work
   176	
   177	About 600 lines of library, 250 of CLI and worker, 400 of tests, one HTML/CSS/TS page. Order: step 0 discovery.ts spike (3.0); arena + labelling + invariants tests; payoff/cycle/vigour pure functions with fixtures; panel pick; calibration (scratch, freeze constants); CLI + worker + evidence/manifest; page; packaging edits and tests; Codex source review. If scope must shrink, cut in this order: `F1` twin control, see the job-schedule cuts in 3.7 (UI cuts do not save simulation time). Optional arms to cut first: F1 twin, rare-arm positions and seeds, contest-time selector.
     1	// Pure helpers for the opponents page: read the saved raw run records and compute the displayed numbers. No DOM lookups,
     2	// no simulator import (payoff.ts is arithmetic only), so every number on the page can be recomputed from evidence.json.
     3	import { blockPayoff, shareA, summarizeInvasion, summarizePair, tInterval, vigourExpected, type Obs } from '../../../../packages/metrics/src/opponents/payoff.ts';
     4	
     5	export interface RunRec { key: string; b0: [number, number]; e0: [number, number]; cp: [number, number, number, number][] }
     6	export interface ViewConfig { ids: string[]; seeds: number[]; rareSeeds: number[]; rarePositions: number }
     7	export type Ratio = 'bal' | 'rare';
     8	export type Position = 'avg' | '1' | '2';
     9	export type Metric = 'payoff' | 'expected' | 'residual';
    10	
    11	export const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]!);
    12	export const f2 = (v: number) => v.toFixed(2);
    13	export const fmt = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString('en-US');
    14	
    15	export type Index = Map<string, RunRec>;
    16	export const indexRuns = (runs: readonly RunRec[]): Index => new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
    17	
    18	export interface CellView {
    19	  state: 'ok' | 'uninformative' | 'none';
    20	  value: number | null;
    21	  lo: number | null;
    22	  hi: number | null;
    23	  n: number;
    24	  total: number;
    25	  excluded: number;
    26	  text: string;
    27	}
    28	const NONE: CellView = { state: 'none', value: null, lo: null, hi: null, n: 0, total: 0, excluded: 0, text: '–' };
    29	
    30	const obs = (r: RunRec, k: number): Obs => ({ b0A: r.b0[0], b0B: r.b0[1], bA: r.cp[k][0], bB: r.cp[k][1] });
    31	
    32	function describe(values: number[], total: number, minFraction: number, signed: boolean): CellView {
    33	  const n = values.length;
    34	  if (n === 0 || n < minFraction * total - 1e-9) return { state: 'uninformative', value: null, lo: null, hi: null, n, total, excluded: total - n, text: `uninformative (${total - n} of ${total} dropped)` };
    35	  const iv = tInterval(values);
    36	  const m = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
    37	  const sign = signed && m > 0 ? '+' : '';
    38	  const val = `${sign}${f2(m)}`;
    39	  const text = iv ? `${val} [${signed && iv.lo > 0 ? '+' : ''}${f2(iv.lo)}, ${signed && iv.hi > 0 ? '+' : ''}${f2(iv.hi)}] n=${n}` : `${val} n=${n}`;
    40	  return { state: 'ok', value: m, lo: iv?.lo ?? null, hi: iv?.hi ?? null, n, total, excluded: total - n, text: total - n ? `${text}, ${total - n} dropped` : text };
    41	}
    42	
    43	/**
    44	 * Cell (row X, column Y). Balanced: X's share of biomass against Y at checkpoint k (1:1 start, both slot sets averaged by default).
    45	 * Rare: change in X's share from its measured starting share when X starts as one of four discs against Y's three.
    46	 */
    47	export function cellView(idx: Index, cfg: ViewConfig, x: string, y: string, ratio: Ratio, pos: Position, k: number): CellView {
    48	  if (x === y) return NONE;
    49	  const first = cfg.ids.indexOf(x) < cfg.ids.indexOf(y);
    50	  const pair = first ? `${x}|${y}` : `${y}|${x}`;
    51	  if (ratio === 'bal') {
    52	    const vals: (number | null)[] = [];
    53	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) {
    54	      const rs = (pos === 'avg' ? ['P1', 'P2'] : [`P${pos}`]).map((p) => idx.get(`c|${pair}|bal|${p}|${seed}`));
    55	      if (rs.some((r) => !r)) return NONE;
    56	      const b = blockPayoff(seed, rs.map((r) => obs(r!, k)));
    57	      vals.push(b.payoff === null ? null : first ? b.payoff : 1 - b.payoff);
    58	    }
    59	    return describe(vals.filter((v): v is number => v !== null), vals.length, 0.8, false);
    60	  }
    61	  if (!cfg.rarePositions) return NONE;
    62	  const arm = first ? 'rA' : 'rB';
    63	  const positions = pos === 'avg' ? (cfg.rarePositions === 2 ? ['R1', 'R2'] : ['R1']) : [`R${pos}`];
    64	  if (positions.some((p) => p === 'R2') && cfg.rarePositions < 2) return NONE;
    65	  const per: (number | null)[] = cfg.rareSeeds.map((seed) => {
    66	    const ex = positions.map((p) => {
    67	      const r = idx.get(`c|${pair}|${arm}|${p}|${seed}`);
    68	      if (!r) return null;
    69	      const o = obs(r, k);
    70	      const sh = first ? shareA(o.b0A, o.b0B, o.bA, o.bB) : shareA(o.b0B, o.b0A, o.bB, o.bA);
    71	      return sh === null ? null : sh - (first ? o.b0A : o.b0B) / (o.b0A + o.b0B);
    72	    });
    73	    return ex.every((v) => v !== null) ? ex.reduce((a, b) => a! + b!, 0)! / ex.length : null;
    74	  });
    75	  return describe(per.filter((v): v is number => v !== null), per.length, 5 / 6, true);
    76	}
    77	
    78	/** Solo-growth prediction of the same cell: the share X would have if its isolated growth at the same positions decided it. */
    79	export function expectedShare(idx: Index, cfg: ViewConfig, x: string, y: string, pos: Position, k: number): number | null {
    80	  const sets: [string, string][] = pos === '1' ? [['m01', 'm23']] : pos === '2' ? [['m23', 'm01']] : [['m01', 'm23'], ['m23', 'm01']];
    81	  const vals: number[] = [];
    82	  for (const seed of cfg.seeds) {
    83	    const e = sets.map(([mx, my]) => {
    84	      const vx = idx.get(`s|${x}|${mx}|${seed}`), vy = idx.get(`s|${y}|${my}|${seed}`);
    85	      return vx && vy ? vigourExpected(vx.cp[k][0], vy.cp[k][0]) : null;
    86	    });
    87	    if (e.every((v) => v !== null)) vals.push(e.reduce((a, b) => a! + b!, 0)! / e.length);
    88	  }
    89	  return vals.length ? vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length : null;
    90	}
    91	
    92	export interface MatrixCell { row: string; col: string; view: CellView; shown: string; expected: number | null; residual: number | null }
    93	export function matrix(idx: Index, cfg: ViewConfig, ratio: Ratio, pos: Position, k: number, metric: Metric): MatrixCell[][] {
    94	  return cfg.ids.map((x) => cfg.ids.map((y) => {
    95	    const view = cellView(idx, cfg, x, y, ratio, pos, k);
    96	    const exp = ratio === 'bal' && x !== y ? expectedShare(idx, cfg, x, y, pos, k) : null;
    97	    const residual = view.value !== null && exp !== null ? view.value - exp : null;
    98	    let shown = view.text;
    99	    if (view.state === 'ok' && ratio === 'bal') {
   100	      if (metric === 'expected') shown = exp === null ? 'no solo prediction' : f2(exp);
   101	      else if (metric === 'residual') shown = residual === null ? 'n/a' : `${residual > 0 ? '+' : ''}${f2(residual)}`;
   102	    }
   103	    return { row: x, col: y, view, shown, expected: exp, residual };
   104	  }));
   105	}
   106	
   107	/** Background for a payoff cell: neutral near 0.5, never a tie colour for uninformative cells. */
   108	export function shadeFor(cell: MatrixCell, ratio: Ratio, metric: Metric): string {
   109	  if (cell.view.state === 'none') return 'transparent';
   110	  if (cell.view.state === 'uninformative') return 'repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#2b281f,#2b281f 6px,#1c1a15 6px,#1c1a15 12px)';
   111	  let d: number | null;
   112	  if (ratio === 'bal') d = metric === 'payoff' ? (cell.view.value ?? 0.5) - 0.5 : metric === 'expected' ? (cell.expected ?? 0.5) - 0.5 : cell.residual;
   113	  else d = cell.view.value;
   114	  if (d === null) return 'transparent';
   115	  const a = Math.min(0.55, Math.abs(d) * 1.1);
   116	  return d >= 0 ? `rgba(124,201,160,${a.toFixed(2)})` : `rgba(214,142,110,${a.toFixed(2)})`;
   117	}
   118	
   119	export interface TimeRow { step: number; text: string }
   120	export function timeRows(idx: Index, cfg: ViewConfig, x: string, y: string, ratio: Ratio, pos: Position, steps: number[]): TimeRow[] {
   121	  return steps.map((step, k) => ({ step, text: cellView(idx, cfg, x, y, ratio, pos, k).text }));
   122	}
   123	
   124	export function invasionText(idx: Index, cfg: ViewConfig, pair: string, arm: 'rA' | 'rB', k: number): string {
   125	  const [a, b] = pair.split('|');
   126	  const x = arm === 'rA' ? a : b, y = arm === 'rA' ? b : a;
   127	  const v = cellView(idx, cfg, x, y, 'rare', 'avg', k);
   128	  if (v.state !== 'ok') return v.text;
   129	  const per = cfg.rareSeeds.map((seed) => {
   130	    const vals = (cfg.rarePositions === 2 ? ['R1', 'R2'] : ['R1']).map((p) => {
   131	      const r = idx.get(`c|${pair}|${arm}|${p}|${seed}`)!;
   132	      const o = obs(r, k);
   133	      const sh = arm === 'rA' ? shareA(o.b0A, o.b0B, o.bA, o.bB) : shareA(o.b0B, o.b0A, o.bB, o.bA);
   134	      return sh === null ? null : sh - (arm === 'rA' ? o.b0A : o.b0B) / (o.b0A + o.b0B);
   135	    });
   136	    return vals.every((q) => q !== null) ? vals.reduce((p, q) => p! + q!, 0)! / vals.length : null;
   137	  });
   138	  return summarizeInvasion(per, Math.ceil((5 / 6) * per.length)).class;
   139	}
   140	
   141	export { summarizePair };
     1	import evidence from '../../../../docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json';
     2	import { esc, f2, indexRuns, matrix, shadeFor, timeRows, type Metric, type Position, type Ratio, type RunRec, type ViewConfig } from './render.ts';
     3	import './style.css';
     4	
     5	const el = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;
     6	const cfg = evidence.config as unknown as ViewConfig;
     7	const idx = indexRuns(evidence.runs as unknown as RunRec[]);
     8	const checkpoints = evidence.constants.checkpoints as number[];
     9	const S = evidence.summary as unknown as {
    10	  twin: { labelInvariance: boolean; positionAdvantage: number[]; signFlipP: number | null; meanAbs: number | null; effectTarget: number; naiveDecisiveRate: number | null; rare: Record<string, number[]> | null } | null;
    11	  weakened: { complete: number; allBelowHalf: boolean; meanPayoffW: number | null; soloAboveFloor: boolean } | null;
    12	  depletion: { bothBelowFloorEveryRun: boolean; status: string; naiveShare: number | null } | null;
    13	  floor: Record<string, Record<string, { contested: number; decisive: number; bothFloor: number; total: number }[]>>;
    14	  matrix: { ids: string[]; cycles: string[][]; breadth: Record<string, number>; meanPayoff: Record<string, number | null> };
    15	};
    16	const gates = evidence.gates as unknown as { gates: Record<string, boolean>; detail: Record<string, unknown> };
    17	const fixtures = evidence.fixtures as unknown as { pass: boolean; rpsSynthetic: { cycles: string[][] }; vigourOnly: { pass: boolean }; bothFloorClassifier: { pass: boolean }; asymmetricExclusion: { pass: boolean } };
    18	
    19	const ratio = el<HTMLSelectElement>('ratio'), pos = el<HTMLSelectElement>('pos'), time = el<HTMLSelectElement>('time'), metric = el<HTMLSelectElement>('metric');
    20	const rowsel = el<HTMLSelectElement>('rowsel'), colsel = el<HTMLSelectElement>('colsel');
    21	const last = checkpoints.length - 1;
    22	
    23	time.innerHTML = checkpoints.map((s, k) => `<option value="${k}"${k === last ? ' selected' : ''}>step ${s}${k === last ? ' (final, the confirmatory time)' : ' (exploratory)'}</option>`).join('');
    24	for (const sel of [rowsel, colsel]) sel.innerHTML = cfg.ids.map((id) => `<option value="${esc(id)}">${esc(id)}</option>`).join('');
    25	colsel.value = cfg.ids[1];
    26	
    27	const VERDICT_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    28	  'assay-sensitive': 'It caught the weakened copy and stayed quiet for identical organisms, at this effect size and resolution.',
    29	  'label-or-position-biased': 'It did not stay quiet for identical organisms (a label or a position effect), so its results cannot be trusted as they stand.',
    30	  insensitive: 'It did not reliably catch the weakened copy.',
    31	  'extinction-limited': 'Too many contests ended with both sides gone to read.',
    32	  'control-invalid': 'A control did not work, so no verdict about the assay can be drawn.',
    33	};
    34	
    35	function render() {
    36	  const r = ratio.value as Ratio, p = pos.value as Position, k = Number(time.value), m = metric.value as Metric;
    37	  const rare = r === 'rare';
    38	  const tr0 = S.twin?.rare?.rA ?? [];
    39	  const twinRare = tr0.length ? tr0.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / tr0.length : null;
    40	  el('mhelp').textContent = rare
    41	    ? `Rare start: each cell is the change in the row organism's share of biomass from the share it started with, when it starts as one disc against three of the column organism. Positive means it gained ground. Exploratory. Read it against the control: the same organism under two names, one disc against three, changed its share by ${twinRare === null ? 'n/a' : (twinRare > 0 ? '+' : '') + f2(twinRare)} on average, so a lone disc is disadvantaged by arrangement alone and a negative cell here is not by itself a failure to invade.`
    42	    : 'Each cell is the share of biomass the row organism holds against the column organism, with a 95% interval across seed blocks, n = blocks kept. 0.50 is an even split. Blocks where either position ended with both sides gone are dropped whole and counted.';
    43	  const mx = matrix(idx, cfg, r, p, k, rare ? 'payoff' : m);
    44	  const caption = `${rare ? 'Change in share' : metric.selectedOptions[0].textContent} at step ${checkpoints[k]}. Rows are the organism whose share is shown.`;
    45	  el('mcap').textContent = caption;
    46	  el('matrix').querySelector('thead')!.innerHTML = `<tr><th scope="col">Row against column</th>${cfg.ids.map((id) => `<th scope="col">${esc(id)}</th>`).join('')}</tr>`;
    47	  el('matrix').querySelector('tbody')!.innerHTML = mx.map((row, i) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(cfg.ids[i])}</th>${row.map((c) => `<td class="cell ${c.view.state}" style="background:${shadeFor(c, r, rare ? 'payoff' : m)}">${c.view.state === 'uninformative' ? '<strong>uninformative</strong><br>' : ''}${esc(c.shown)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('');
    48	  const tr = timeRows(idx, cfg, rowsel.value, colsel.value, r, p, checkpoints);
    49	  el('timetable').querySelector('tbody')!.innerHTML = rowsel.value === colsel.value ? '<tr><td colspan="2">Pick two different organisms.</td></tr>' : tr.map((t) => `<tr><th scope="row">${t.step}${t.step === checkpoints[last] ? ' (final)' : ''}</th><td>${esc(t.text)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    50	}
    51	
    52	function renderStatic() {
    53	  const v = evidence.verdict as string;
    54	  const ok = v === 'assay-sensitive';
    55	  el('verdict').className = `verdict ${ok ? 'good' : 'warn'}`;
    56	  el('verdict').textContent = `Verdict recorded in the evidence: ${v}. ${VERDICT_TEXT[v] ?? ''} A non-sensitive verdict is a valid result and is shown as it came out.`;
    57	  const t = S.twin, w = S.weakened, d = S.depletion, g = gates.gates;
    58	  const rows: [string, string, string, boolean][] = [
    59	    ['Same organism, two names: exact', 'Swapping the names changes nothing physical: counts swap exactly.', t ? (t.labelInvariance ? 'counts swap exactly in every seed and time' : 'counts differ') : 'not run', g.G1a],
    60	    ['Same organism, two names: position', `No systematic gain from where it is planted (sign-flip p above 0.05) and the mean size of the place effect below ${t?.effectTarget ?? '?'}.`, t ? `place effect per seed: ${t.positionAdvantage.map((x) => (x > 0 ? '+' : '') + f2(x)).join(', ')}; mean size ${t?.meanAbs === null ? '–' : f2(t!.meanAbs!)}; p = ${t?.signFlipP === null ? '–' : t!.signFlipP!.toFixed(3)}. A naive reading calls ${t?.naiveDecisiveRate === null ? '–' : Math.round(100 * t!.naiveDecisiveRate!)}% of single runs decisive.` : 'not run', g.G1b],
    61	    ['Weakened copy must lose', 'Photosynthesis weight cut by half: it loses to its parent in every block and holds at most 0.40, and it is alive when growing alone.', w ? `${w.complete} complete blocks; mean share ${w.meanPayoffW === null ? '–' : f2(w.meanPayoffW)}; alive alone: ${w.soloAboveFloor ? 'yes' : 'no'}` : 'not run', g.G2],
    62	    ['Not too many empty contests', 'At most 20% of contests with both sides gone, in every matchup and start; no matchup unreadable.', JSON.stringify(gates.detail.floorFails) === '[]' && JSON.stringify(gates.detail.uninformativePairs) === '[]' ? 'within limits' : `over limit: ${JSON.stringify(gates.detail.floorFails)} unreadable: ${JSON.stringify(gates.detail.uninformativePairs)}`, g.G3],
    63	    ['Both sides gone is not a tie', 'A darkened, decaying tile must end with both sides at the floor and be called unreadable.', d ? `${d.bothBelowFloorEveryRun ? 'both sides at the floor in every run' : 'not every run reached the floor'}; status ${d.status}; a naive reading says a ${d.naiveShare === null ? '–' : f2(d.naiveShare)} tie` : 'no in-world arena: classifier fixture only', g.G4],
    64	    ['Constructed fixtures', 'Growth-only advantage has zero residual; a built A>B>C>A cycle is found and a transitive set is not; dropped blocks are handled whole.', `cycle found in the synthetic fixture: ${JSON.stringify(fixtures.rpsSynthetic.cycles)}`, g.G5],
    65	  ];
    66	  el('controls').querySelector('tbody')!.innerHTML = rows.map(([a, b, c, ok2]) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(a)}</th><td>${esc(b)}</td><td>${esc(c)}</td><td>${ok2 ? 'yes' : '<strong>no</strong>'}</td></tr>`).join('');
    67	  const ext: string[] = [];
    68	  for (const [pr, arms] of Object.entries(S.floor)) for (const [arm, cps] of Object.entries(arms)) {
    69	    const c = cps[last];
    70	    ext.push(`<tr><th scope="row">${esc(pr)}</th><td>${arm === 'bal' ? '1:1' : arm === 'rA' ? 'first rare' : 'second rare'}</td><td>${c.contested}</td><td>${c.decisive}</td><td>${c.bothFloor}</td></tr>`);
    71	  }
    72	  el('ext').querySelector('tbody')!.innerHTML = ext.join('');
    73	  el('cycles').textContent = S.matrix.cycles.length ? `Cycles among confirmed edges: ${S.matrix.cycles.map((c) => c.join(' > ') + ' > ' + c[0]).join('; ')}.` : 'Cycles among confirmed edges: none detected at this resolution. The detector finds a cycle on a built, synthetic A>B>C>A fixture; "none detected" here does not mean none exist.';
    74	  el('breadth').querySelector('tbody')!.innerHTML = S.matrix.ids.map((id) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(id)}</th><td>${S.matrix.meanPayoff[id] === null ? '–' : f2(S.matrix.meanPayoff[id]!)}</td><td>${S.matrix.breadth[id]}</td></tr>`).join('');
    75	}
    76	
    77	for (const s of [ratio, pos, time, metric, rowsel, colsel]) s.addEventListener('change', render);
    78	renderStatic();
    79	render();

    22	/**
    23	 * A request's path, decoded and normalised (`.` and `..` segments resolved, repeated slashes folded, never above the
    24	 * root), its raw form, and its query; null when the path does not decode. Every check below reads the normalised path,
    25	 * so `//404.html`, `/./404.html` or `/x/../404.html` are the not-found page by name too, and no existence check looks
    26	 * outside the root.
    27	 */
    28	function splitUrl(url = "/"): { pathname: string; decoded: string; path: string; search: string } | null {
    29	  const q = url.indexOf("?");
    30	  const [pathname, search] = q < 0 ? [url, ""] : [url.slice(0, q), url.slice(q)];
    31	  try {
    32	    const decoded = decodeURIComponent(pathname);
    33	    return { pathname, decoded, path: posix.normalize(posix.join("/", decoded)), search };
    34	  } catch {
    35	    return null;
    36	  }
    37	}
    38	
    39	/**
    40	 * The not-found page asked for by name (`/404.html`, `/404.html/`, `/404%2ehtml`, `/404.HTML`): it answers 404 too, as in
    41	 * Caddy, whose path matcher ignores case. Case is ignored here as well, since a case-insensitive disk (macOS) would
    42	 * otherwise serve `/404.HTML` with 200.
    43	 */
    44	const namesNotFound = (path: string) => path.replace(/\/+$/, "").toLowerCase() === "/404.html";
    45	
    46	/** The queries Vite's module loaders read (`?raw`, `?url`, `?import`, `?html-proxy` and the like). */
    47	const VITE_MODULE_QUERY = /[?&](?:import|raw|url|inline|html-proxy|worker|sharedworker|direct)(?:[=&]|$)/;
    48	/** Fetch destinations Vite's transform middleware never answers (vite 8, middlewares/transform.ts). */
    49	const DOCUMENT_DESTS = new Set(["document", "iframe", "frame", "fencedframe"]);
    50	
    51	/**
    52	 * A module request for an .html file, which Vite's transform middleware answers as JavaScript: a module query, from a
    53	 * fetch that is not a document's, that Vite would transform (a script fetch, an `import` query or an `html-proxy` one,
    54	 * the same tests its middleware makes). Anything else asking for the page by name is a navigation or a plain fetch,
    55	 * which a static server would answer with the file and 200, so it gets the page.
    56	 */
    57	function viteModuleRequest(req: Connect.IncomingMessage, search: string): boolean {
    58	  const dest = req.headers["sec-fetch-dest"];
    59	  if (typeof dest === "string" && DOCUMENT_DESTS.has(dest)) return false;
    60	  if (!VITE_MODULE_QUERY.test(search)) return false;
    61	  return dest === "script" || /[?&]import=?(?:&|$)/.test(search) || /[?&]html-proxy\b/.test(search);
    62	}
    63	
    64	/**
    65	 * An unknown page answers 404 with the site's own page, 404.html, as deploy/Caddyfile does in production. Runs after
    66	 * Vite's mpa html fallback, which has already rewritten a known page to its .html file. Only requests Vite's fallback
    67	 * would treat as a page (GET or HEAD, with an Accept that names text/html or *\/* or is absent) get the page; a fetch
    68	 * asking for JSON keeps Vite's empty 404. A directory with an index.html asked for without its slash answers 308 to
    69	 * the slash, keeping the query, which is what Caddy's `redir` lines do for the eight top-level pages. Known differences
    70	 * from Caddy: dev sends any such directory to its slash (`/garden`, which Caddy serves at 200 without one, and dev-only
    71	 * directories such as `/dist`); Vite also answers `/<name>` with `/<name>.html` when that file exists (`/selftest`);
    72	 * dev has no `/<page>/world/<id>/` copies; a path that does not decode (`/%zz`) gets the page here, where Caddy
    73	 * answers its own empty 400; and on a case-blind disk (macOS) a page asked for in another case is found (`/ABOUT/`
    74	 * answers 200 in dev and preview, `/Lab/index.HTML` 200 in dev), where production's Linux disk answers 404, and `/About`
    75	 * is sent to `/About/` where Caddy's case-blind `redir` sends it to `/about/`. Only the not-found page's own name is
    76	 * matched without case here.
    77	 */
    78	function notFound(root: string, page: () => Promise<string>): Connect.NextHandleFunction {
    79	  return (req, res, next) => {
    80	    const url = splitUrl(req.url);
    81	    const named = url !== null && namesNotFound(url.path);
    82	    const accept = req.headers.accept;
    83	    const asPage = (req.method === "GET" || req.method === "HEAD") && (accept === undefined || accept === "" || accept.includes("text/html") || accept.includes("*/*"));
    84	    if (!named && !asPage) return next();
    85	    if (url && !named && url.path.endsWith(".html") && existsSync(join(root, url.path))) return next();
    86	    // Only a path that is already in normal form is redirected, to itself plus a slash: `//name` (another host) or a
    87	    // path with dot segments gets the page instead.
    88	    if (url && !named && url.decoded === url.path && !url.path.endsWith("/") && existsSync(join(root, url.path, "index.html"))) {
    89	      res.writeHead(308, { Location: `${url.pathname}/${url.search}` }).end();
    90	      return;
    91	    }
    92	    page().then((html) => {
    93	      res.writeHead(404, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
    94	      res.end(req.method === "HEAD" ? undefined : html);
    95	    }, next);
    96	  };
    97	}
    98	
    99	/**
   100	 * The static servers would answer the not-found page asked for by name with 200, so those requests are taken first. In
   101	 * dev, a module request for it (`import text from "/404.html?raw"`, which Vite sends as `?raw&import`) still goes on
   102	 * to Vite's transforms, which answer it as JavaScript; a navigation or plain fetch with the same query does not, since
   103	 * Vite would not transform it and its static files would serve `/404.HTML?raw` with 200 on a case-blind disk.
   104	 * `vite preview` has no transforms, so there every request for it is taken.
   105	 */
   106	const namedNotFound = (handle: Connect.NextHandleFunction, { modules }: { modules: boolean }): Connect.NextHandleFunction => (req, res, next) => {
   107	  const url = splitUrl(req.url);
   108	  if (!url || !namesNotFound(url.path)) return next();
   109	  return modules && viteModuleRequest(req, url.search) ? next() : handle(req, res, next);
   110	};

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cellView, esc, expectedShare, indexRuns, matrix, shadeFor, timeRows, type RunRec, type ViewConfig } from '../src/opponents/render.ts';

const cfg: ViewConfig = { ids: ['A', 'B'], seeds: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], rareSeeds: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], rarePositions: 1 };
const run = (key: string, b0: [number, number], cps: [number, number][]): RunRec => ({ key, b0, e0: [0, 0], cp: cps.map(([a, b]) => [a, b, 1, 1]) });
function runs(over: Record<string, [number, number][]> = {}): RunRec[] {
  const out: RunRec[] = [];
  for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const pos of ['P1', 'P2']) {
    const k = `c|A|B|bal|${pos}|${seed}`;
    out.push(run(k, [1000, 1000], over[k] ?? [[700, 300], [800, 200]]));
  }
  for (const seed of cfg.rareSeeds) for (const arm of ['rA', 'rB']) {
    const k = `c|A|B|${arm}|R1|${seed}`;
    out.push(run(k, arm === 'rA' ? [500, 1500] : [1500, 500], over[k] ?? [[500, 1500], [500, 1500]]));
  }
  for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const m of ['m01', 'm23']) for (const id of ['A', 'B']) out.push(run(`s|${id}|${m}|${seed}`, [1000, 0], [[id === 'A' ? 3000 : 1000, 0], [id === 'A' ? 3000 : 1000, 0]]));
  return out;
}

it('matrix numbers equal the raw records; mirrored cells are complements', () => {
  const idx = indexRuns(runs());
  const ab = cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', 'avg', 1), ba = cellView(idx, cfg, 'B', 'A', 'bal', 'avg', 1);
  expect(ab.value).toBeCloseTo(0.8);
  expect(ba.value).toBeCloseTo(0.2);
  expect(ab.text).toContain('0.80');
  expect(cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'A', 'bal', 'avg', 1).state).toBe('none');
});

it('uninformative cells are labelled, not coloured as ties', () => {
  const over: Record<string, [number, number][]> = {};
  for (const seed of [1, 2, 3]) over[`c|A|B|bal|P1|${seed}`] = [[1, 1], [1, 1]];
  const idx = indexRuns(runs(over));
  const c = cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', 'avg', 1);
  expect(c.state).toBe('uninformative');
  expect(c.text).toContain('uninformative');
  const m = matrix(idx, cfg, 'bal', 'avg', 1, 'payoff');
  expect(shadeFor(m[0][1], 'bal', 'payoff')).toContain('repeating-linear-gradient');
  expect(shadeFor(m[0][1], 'bal', 'payoff')).not.toContain('rgba(124');
});

it('checkpoint, ratio and position toggles change the displayed numbers', () => {
  const over: Record<string, [number, number][]> = {};
  for (const seed of cfg.seeds) over[`c|A|B|bal|P2|${seed}`] = [[400, 600], [500, 500]];
  const idx = indexRuns(runs(over));
  const t0 = cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', 'avg', 0).text, t1 = cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', 'avg', 1).text;
  expect(t0).not.toBe(t1);
  expect(cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', '1', 1).value).toBeCloseTo(0.8);
  expect(cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', '2', 1).value).toBeCloseTo(0.5);
  const rare = cellView(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'rare', 'avg', 1);
  expect(rare.value).toBeCloseTo(0.25 - 0.25);
  expect(timeRows(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'bal', 'avg', [10, 20]).map((r) => r.text)).toEqual([t0, t1]);
});

it('growing-alone prediction and residual follow the isolated runs', () => {
  const idx = indexRuns(runs());
  expect(expectedShare(idx, cfg, 'A', 'B', 'avg', 1)).toBeCloseTo(0.75);
  const m = matrix(idx, cfg, 'bal', 'avg', 1, 'residual');
  expect(m[0][1].residual).toBeCloseTo(0.8 - 0.75);
});

  expect(esc('<a href="x">&')).toBe('&lt;a href=&quot;x&quot;&gt;&amp;');
  const html = readFileSync(new URL('../opponents/index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
  expect(html).toMatch(/synthetic/);
  const css = readFileSync(new URL('../src/opponents/style.css', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
  expect(css).toContain('prefers-reduced-motion');
});

      <h1>Can a head-to-head contest tell a few known organisms apart?</h1>
      <p>Five organisms, each planted in small discs on a small tile, two at a time, with mutation switched off. The score is who holds more biomass after a fixed time. Before trusting that score, we check it against three things it must get right: a deliberately weakened copy must lose, the same organism under two names must not win by name or by position, and a contest where both sides die out must be called unreadable, not a tie.</p>
      <p class="disclosure">CPU only: small reference-simulator tiles, no GPU evidence. Local experiment, kept off the public site. This is a sensitivity check, not a tournament and not the M3 environment.</p>

      <h2 id="caption-heading">What this does not show</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>A sensitivity check on five genetically separated organisms in small CPU tiles. It is not the M3 environment: one fixed chemistry, mutation off, a few hundred steps.</li>
        <li>A result is the share of biomass held at a step. It is not reproduction or fitness, and a win can simply be vigour: see the growing-alone view.</li>
        <li>The cycle test was proved on a synthetic A-beats-B-beats-C-beats-A fixture. "None detected" on the real organisms is not "none exist".</li>
        <li>Only the final-time, even-start numbers are confirmatory. Other times, the rare start, and the growing-alone residual are exploratory and decide nothing.</li>
        <li>An interval that contains an even split is not evidence of equality. Ten seed blocks give wide intervals.</li>
        <li>The weakened copy is one manipulation, a sensitivity floor and not a spectrum of effect sizes.</li>
        <li>No archive of past generations exists yet, so there is no time-versus-opponent history and no tournament. Contest time here means steps inside one contest.</li>
      </ul>
      <p class="muted">CPU only, no GPU evidence, not on the public site. No score here selected anything.</p>
    </section>
  </main>
  <script type="module" src="/src/opponents/page.ts"></script>
</body>
</html>

     1	# I03 first rung: implementation evidence (2026-11-01)
     2	
     3	Branch `claude/i03-historical-opponents` from `origin/main` 67fdd025. Uncommitted. CPU only (sim-ref), no GPU evidence, not the M3 environment.
     4	
     5	## Result
     6	
     7	`evidence.json` verdict: **assay-sensitive**, defined as: detects the constructed weakening at this effect size and stays quiet for identical genomes at this resolution, on genetically separated founders in small CPU arenas. It does not assert discrimination between real strategies. Seal `9f43b866...7aef` (identical across two independent `--write` runs and `--replay-check`).
     8	
     9	Gates (all pass): G1a exact label invariance; G1b twin position effect (10 seeds, mean size 0.066 < target 0.075, sign-flip p = 0.68; per-seed effects range -0.11..+0.20, so single runs are noisy and the pass is near its target); G2 W loses to G in all 10 blocks, W mean share 0.347, W alive alone; G3 no both-floor runs in any pair or arm; G4 in-world depletion arena (dark, kBDecay 600, kELeak 1000) both sides at floor in all 20 runs, status uninformative, naive reading is a 0.50 tie; G5 fixtures.
    10	
    11	Real panel (G, three farthest-point M3 founders F1..F3, W): confirmed edges G>F3, G>W (G vs F1 is even, 0.477), F1>F2, F1>F3, F1>W. Cycles: none detected at this resolution (the detector finds the constructed A>B>C>A fixture only). The panel is ordered F1 ~ G > F2 > W ~ F3 by mean payoff; edges are strict (all 10 blocks one side, p <= 0.005, gap >= 0.05).
    12	
    13	## Findings worth stating
    14	
    15	- Exploratory rare-start (1 disc vs 3): the rare label loses share in nearly every pair, including the identical-genome control (-0.16 mean, all 6 seeds). A lone disc is disadvantaged by arrangement alone, so "fails" classes in the rare arm are not interpretable as failure to invade; read against the twin rare row. Not gated.
    16	- Vigour residual (descriptive): residuals are within about +/-0.09 of solo-growth predictions; the largest is F1 vs F3 (+0.089). No causal separation claimed.
    17	- Calibration: first attainable setting in cost order was attempt 11 (tile 32, R 3, T 300, weaken 50%); the twin target was met on 3 calibration seeds by a thin margin (0.0752 vs 0.0755). All 11 attempts and 3 depletion candidates are in `calibration.json`.
    18	
    19	## Step 0 spike (plan 3.0): discovery.ts
    20	
    21	`packages/runner/src/discovery.ts` was read for fixture vocabulary only. Its runner is not reused: it has no per-lineage payoff observer (adding one is a shared runner/schema change) and writes a checkpoint artifact per case, heavy for ~724 throwaway contests. Contest worlds are expressed as founder specs with genome hex so a later rung can port them. Plan unchanged.
    22	
    23	## Cost
    24	
    25	724 runs (484 contests, 220 solo, 20 depletion), 300 steps, 32x32. About 1290 CPU-seconds. On a heavily loaded machine (load average 25-80, 8 workers) `--write` took 219 s and 396 s, `--replay-check` 466 s; the "under about 5 minutes" budget is met only on an unloaded machine (about 160 s ideal on 8 cores). Not qualified under load. Cuts per plan 3.7 if needed: rare arms to one position and fewer seeds first.
    26

    31	- `node tools/opponents/assay.ts --check`: ok. `--replay-check --jobs 8`: ok (7:45 wall under load).

// Small statistics toolkit for ensemble analysis (no dependencies).

export function mean(xs: number[]): number {
  return xs.length ? xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length : NaN;
}

export function sd(xs: number[]): number {
  if (xs.length < 2) return NaN;
  const m = mean(xs);
  return Math.sqrt(xs.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - m) ** 2, 0) / (xs.length - 1));
}

export function pearson(xs: number[], ys: number[]): number {
  const n = Math.min(xs.length, ys.length);
  if (n < 3) return NaN;
  const mx = mean(xs.slice(0, n)), my = mean(ys.slice(0, n));
  let sxy = 0, sxx = 0, syy = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    sxy += (xs[i] - mx) * (ys[i] - my);
    sxx += (xs[i] - mx) ** 2;
    syy += (ys[i] - my) ** 2;
  }
  return sxx > 0 && syy > 0 ? sxy / Math.sqrt(sxx * syy) : NaN;
}

export interface LinearFit {
  slope: number;
  intercept: number;
  sse: number;
  r2: number;
}

export function linearFit(t: number[], y: number[]): LinearFit {
  const n = t.length;
  const mt = mean(t), my = mean(y);
  let stt = 0, sty = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    stt += (t[i] - mt) ** 2;
    sty += (t[i] - mt) * (y[i] - my);
  }
  const slope = stt > 0 ? sty / stt : 0;
  const intercept = my - slope * mt;
  let sse = 0, sst = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    sse += (y[i] - (intercept + slope * t[i])) ** 2;

Recorded verdict: **`assay-sensitive`**. I found six P2 issues and one P3. No files were edited.

1. **P2 — Position-specific residuals use the wrong solo masks.** [render.ts:80](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/apps/lab/src/opponents/render.ts:80)  
   `cellView` complements lower-triangle results using the stored pair’s orientation, but `expectedShare` assigns masks directly from row order. In the current evidence, step 300/P1, **F2 against F1 displays +0.03 residual; the correct value is −0.02**.  
   **Fix:** derive the expectation in canonical pair order and complement reversed cells. Test unequal solo growth across masks.

2. **P2 — G3 ignores incomplete rare-arm blocks.** [evidence.ts:360](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:360)  
   Only balanced-arm eligibility enters `uninformativePairs`. Two both-floor runs in different rare-A blocks leave **4/6 complete blocks**, but represent only **2/12 runs**, below the 20% floor threshold. I reproduced `rareA.class = "uninformative"` while G3 and `assay-sensitive` remained true. This violates PLAN’s eligibility requirement for each arm.  
   **Fix:** gate complete-block eligibility separately for every enabled pair/arm.

3. **P2 — Impossible raw records pass verification.** [evidence.ts:176](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:176), [evidence.ts:368](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:368)  
   Validation checks schedule membership, while `invalid` is hardcoded false. Changing a recorded cell count to **−42**, resealing, and updating manifest hashes still makes `checkSaved()` return `[]`. Extra checkpoint tuples are also ignored.  
   **Fix:** validate tuple lengths, checkpoint counts, finite nonnegative integer inventories, positive contest inocula, and physical cell-count bounds before aggregation; reject invalid evidence.

4. **P2 — `--check` trusts frozen metadata that it could reconstruct.** [assay.ts:125](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/tools/opponents/assay.ts:125), [evidence.ts:429](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:429)

   **Fix:** reconstruct panel metadata, constants, checkpoint steps and schedule from current frozen inputs; compare them with both artifacts.

5. **P2 — The prospective calibration freeze is missing.** [EVIDENCE.md:38](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/EVIDENCE.md:38)

This is a provenance gap, not proof of tuning.  
   **Fix:** describe these results as exploratory unless contemporaneous freeze evidence exists; otherwise freeze the corrected protocol before collecting fresh confirmatory seeds.

6. **P2 — Excluded contests still influence displayed residuals.** [render.ts:82](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/apps/lab/src/opponents/render.ts:82)  
   Observed payoff drops incomplete blocks, but solo expectation averages every seed. I constructed nine eligible blocks with observed and expected shares both 0.5, plus one excluded block whose solo expectation is 1. The page displays **−0.05**, although every retained block has zero residual. The backend correctly pairs these quantities.  
   **Fix:** calculate the page’s expectation and residual over exactly the retained contest blocks.

   **Fix:** validate tuple lengths, checkpoint counts, finite nonnegative integer inventories, positive contest inocula, and physical cell-count bounds before aggregation; reject invalid evidence.

4. **P2 — `--check` trusts frozen metadata that it could reconstruct.** [assay.ts:125](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/tools/opponents/assay.ts:125), [evidence.ts:429](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:429)  
   In-memory resealed variants all passed `checkSaved()` with unchanged source hashes: W’s genome replaced by G’s, schedule count changed to one, and the final checkpoint relabelled **30000** in constants and summary. The checker recomputes statistics but trusts their identifying metadata.  
   **Fix:** reconstruct panel metadata, constants, checkpoint steps and schedule from current frozen inputs; compare them with both artifacts.

5. **P2 — The prospective calibration freeze is missing.** [EVIDENCE.md:38](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/EVIDENCE.md:38)  
   PLAN requires committing calibration before the first evidence write. Everything remains untracked, evidence has already been generated repeatedly, and this paragraph proposes making calibration precede evidence “in history.” Later commit ordering cannot establish that thresholds were frozen before outcomes were observed. This is a provenance gap, not proof of tuning.  
   **Fix:** describe these results as exploratory unless contemporaneous freeze evidence exists; otherwise freeze the corrected protocol before collecting fresh confirmatory seeds.

6. **P2 — Excluded contests still influence displayed residuals.** [render.ts:82](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/apps/lab/src/opponents/render.ts:82)  
   Observed payoff drops incomplete blocks, but solo expectation averages every seed. I constructed nine eligible blocks with observed and expected shares both 0.5, plus one excluded block whose solo expectation is 1. The page displays **−0.05**, although every retained block has zero residual. The backend correctly pairs these quantities.  
   **Fix:** calculate the page’s expectation and residual over exactly the retained contest blocks.

7. **P3 — Absence of triangles is labelled transitivity.** [payoff.ts:137](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/payoff.ts:137), [evidence.ts:237](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-historical-opponents/packages/metrics/src/opponents/evidence.ts:237)  
   A sparse **A>B>C>D>A** graph returns no three-cycles and `transitive: true`. Confirmatory edge graphs can be incomplete, so the triangle test does not establish transitivity.  
   **Fix:** implement the promised transitivity check or rename the field `noThreeCycle`.
